=== RUN   TestRunMapsExitCodes/help
amq-squad - role-aware agent team launcher on top of AMQ

Usage:
  amq-squad <command> [options]

Commands:
  roles        List built-in role IDs and market numbers for team creation
  role         Draft and validate reusable custom role personas
  brief        Draft or seed the workstream brief (the only drafter on any launch-capable path)
  team         Propose, apply and show the squad (propose, apply, show, check, draft-rules)
  task         Task store with leases and pools (add, claim, done, list, reconcile)
  worktree     Plan, materialize, inspect, hand off, and safely clean worker worktrees
  evidence     Run and inspect immutable task-scoped command evidence; ingest hook payloads
  context      Hook verb: the seat's role, rules digest, current task and open gates
  guard        Hook verb: one verdict (allow, needs_gate, deny) for one tool call
  seat-exec    Seat wrapper run by AMQ's launch API: verify, set identity and role, exec the provider
  seat         Harness steps for one seat: the commit step on a done report (seat commit), the owner's clear (seat clear)
  scheduler    The squad's scheduler process (scheduler run), and seat events from an external runner (scheduler post)
  launch       Start a squad's seats through the AMQ launch API (one request, seat-exec per seat)
  verify       Deterministic preflight checks (action, signed authorization, merge, release)
  merge-train  Land the lead's ordered PR set under the merge grant (checks, digest, audit log)
  release      The release gate flow: prepare the tag and release gates, publish after the owner's answers
  gate         Manage durable typed authorization requests (raise and close)
  operator     Inspect or act as the configured operator participant
  status       Show the team's live state in one workstream (--project, --profile, --session)
  fleet        The fleet registry ~/.amq-squad/fleet.yaml (list, add, remove, operator)
  routing      Routing table report: per class, tier and size band, one proposed change per bucket (report --week)
  dispatch     Queue a durable task for a child
  amq          Show the resolved AMQ context and explain routes (env, route)
  doctor       Probe the host: tools, versions, logins, registry, local models, trust and owner config
  trust        Print the owner-run steps to trust a repository in codex-cli (writes nothing)
  lead         Start the lead on the built-in pi (solo in this directory, or the team lead)
  pi           The built-in pi runtime (run a pi seat, verify the unpacked runtime)
  doctor       Check amq-squad / AMQ setup (use --project and --profile for other teams)
  remote       Buzz attachment: bind the enrolled share, attach it to a mailbox, show presence
  version      Print the amq-squad version

Global flags (accepted before or after the subcommand, until a literal "--"):
  --quiet              Suppress non-data success/progress notices.
  --verbose            Print additional diagnostic detail.
  --color auto|always|never
                       Control ANSI color output (default auto; honors NO_COLOR).

Exit codes:
  0  success
  1  usage / user error (unknown flag, bad argument, missing required input)
  2  system / runtime error (IO, process, config, environment)
  3  partial success (some targets succeeded, some failed)

Examples:
  amq-squad roles
  amq-squad team propose --roles cto,fullstack --runtime fullstack=codex --lead cto
  amq-squad team check
  amq-squad status --project ~/Code/app --session issue-435 --json
  amq-squad dispatch --session issue-96 --role qa --subject "Validate" --body-file task.md
  amq-squad task list --session issue-96
  amq-squad context --me cto --json
  amq-squad guard --me cto --stdin --json < pretooluse.json
  amq-squad doctor --project ~/Code/app --json | jq .
  amq-squad trust ~/Code/app
  amq-squad lead --model ollama/qwen3.6:27b

Run 'amq-squad <command> --help' for command-specific options.

=== RUN   TestRunMapsExitCodes/unknown_flag_on_doctor_->_usage
flag provided but not defined: -banana
amq-squad doctor - probe this host for amq-squad 3.0 (plan 5 and 16.2)

Usage:
  amq-squad doctor [--project DIR] [--json] [--write-registry] [--probe [--runs N]] [--usage-scan DUR] [--fix]

Checks: the AMQ version and launch API, the amq-squad on PATH vs this build,
the AMQ identity pin and ops; then the tools on PATH (claude, codex, the
built-in pi, grok, cursor-agent, ollama, amq, amq-remote, tmux, gh) with their
versions against the versions the AMQ launch API verified and their login
state, RAM and GPU, the registry entries, the full re-hash of the embedded pi
runtime, sleep and the amq-remote login service, project roots codex-cli does
not trust, the Codex notify fan-out line, exported key names in the shell
profile, the owner's global agent config against the preflight copy, and the
grants file mode. A missing optional tool is one status line, not a failure.
Without --write-registry, --probe or --fix (pi runtime repair) doctor writes nothing. Exits non-zero if
any check is "fail".

Examples:
  amq-squad doctor
  amq-squad doctor --project ~/Code/app
  amq-squad doctor --write-registry
  amq-squad doctor --probe --runs 5
  amq-squad doctor --usage-scan 168h
  amq-squad doctor --json | jq '.data.checks[] | select(.status=="fail")'
